output.md
Done. The cardio log logic is implemented, green, and committed as `[bf2597d](https://example.com/commit/bf2597d)`.

What changed:
- [CardioLogFields.kt](/work/shared/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/gymie/shared/cardio/CardioLogFields.kt): `CardioLogField` enum and `CardioActivity.logFields` in sheet order for all six activities (indoor ride = outdoor ride minus elevation; treadmill = outdoor run minus elevation and cadence). The backend `CardioActivity.kt` it should mirror was not present in this repo, so that cross-check could not be done here.
- [CardioLogDraft.kt](/work/shared/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/gymie/shared/cardio/CardioLogDraft.kt): immutable `CardioLogDraft` data class (`effort`, `note` are `copy()`-able constructor properties) with text/with/left/shownError/errors/canSubmit/derived/toRequest plus `fresh` (clock prefill floored to the second when in progress) and `of` (saved log as text, effort from it, note from workout notes; saved derived-only pace stays blank so it recomputes). Duration/pace parse `m:ss` and `h:mm:ss` with 00–59 after a colon; errors are `use h:mm:ss`, `max heart rate is below average`, and `between X and Y <unit>`; rower distances are metres and derived pace is per 500 m via `CardioFormat`.
- [CardioLogDraftTest.kt](/work/shared/src/commonTest/kotlin/com/gymie/shared/cardio/CardioLogDraftTest.kt): 14 tests covering the specified behaviors (33:12→1992, 1:12:40→4360, derived 6:37/31.9/2:06, the 41:06 clock prefill, 14:29 recompute, rower metres, and the exact error texts).

Commands run, all passing:
- `./gradlew :shared:desktopTest --tests '*CardioLogDraftTest*'` — 14 tests, 0 failures.
- `./gradlew :shared:ktlintFormat` then `./gradlew :shared:ktlintCheck :shared:detekt :shared:desktopTest --tests 'CardioLogDraftTest'` — BUILD SUCCESSFUL (this caught and I fixed two detekt findings: too many class functions and return count in the clock parser, by moving helpers to top level).

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.clock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.hourClock
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlin.math.roundToInt
+import kotlin.math.roundToLong
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+private const val SECONDS_PER_MINUTE = 60
+private const val SECONDS_PER_HOUR = 3600
+private const val MAX_DURATION_SECONDS = 86400
+private const val MIN_HEART_RATE = 30
+private const val MAX_HEART_RATE_VALUE = 250
+private const val MAX_CALORIES = 20000
+private const val MAX_ELEVATION_M = 20000
+private const val MIN_PACE_SECONDS = 60
+private const val MAX_PACE_SECONDS = 3600
+private const val MAX_ROWER_PACE_SECONDS = 600
+private const val MIN_SPEED = 0.1
+private const val MAX_SPEED = 120.0
+private const val MIN_CADENCE = 1
+private const val MAX_CADENCE = 250
+private const val MIN_ROWER_CADENCE = 10
+private const val MAX_ROWER_CADENCE = 60
+private const val MIN_POWER = 1
+private const val MAX_POWER = 2500
+private const val MAX_DISTANCE_KM = 1000.0
+private const val MIN_EFFORT = 1
+private const val MAX_EFFORT = 10
+private const val TEN = 10.0
+
+/**
+ * The log sheet's typed values (KAN-939 `client-ranges`, `derived-never-sent`): raw text per field,
+ * [effort] and [note] as values, and the fields the user has left. Immutable; every edit returns a
+ * copy. The 400 ms debounce lives in the sheet, not here.
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+    val units: DistanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+    val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val left: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+    val effort: Int? = null,
+    val note: String = "",
+) {
+    /** What is typed for [field], "" when nothing is typed. */
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> texts[field] ?: effort?.toString() ?: ""
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> note
+            else -> texts[field] ?: ""
+        }
+
+    /** [value] typed into [field]. */
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> {
+                copy(texts = texts + (field to value), effort = value.toIntOrNull())
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> {
+                copy(note = value)
+            }
+
+            else -> {
+                copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+            }
+        }
+
+    /** Marks [field] as left, so its error may show. */
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(left = left + field)
+
+    /** `errors()[field]` once the field was left, else null. */
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (field in left) errors()[field] else null
+
+    /**
+     * Every typed value outside the backend's `CardioLogSpecification` ranges (rower cadence 10–60
+     * per `client-ranges`); an empty field is never in error.
+     */
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> {
+        val parsed = parsed()
+        val out = mutableMapOf<CardioLogField, String>()
+        durationError(text(CardioLogField.DURATION), parsed.durationSeconds)?.let {
+            out[CardioLogField.DURATION] = it
+        }
+        distanceError(text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE), parsed.distance, activity, units)?.let {
+            out[CardioLogField.DISTANCE] = it
+        }
+        paceError(text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE), parsed.paceSeconds, activity, units)?.let {
+            out[CardioLogField.AVG_PACE] = it
+        }
+        speedError(text(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED), parsed.speed, units)?.let {
+            out[CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED] = it
+        }
+        intError(
+            text(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN),
+            parsed.elevation,
+            0,
+            MAX_ELEVATION_M,
+            elevationUnit(units),
+        )?.let { out[CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN] = it }
+        intError(
+            text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE),
+            parsed.avgHeartRate,
+            MIN_HEART_RATE,
+            MAX_HEART_RATE_VALUE,
+            "bpm",
+        )?.let { out[CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE] = it }
+        intError(
+            text(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE),
+            parsed.maxHeartRate,
+            MIN_HEART_RATE,
+            MAX_HEART_RATE_VALUE,
+            "bpm",
+        )?.let { out[CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE] = it }
+        if (out[CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE] == null &&
+            parsed.avgHeartRate != null &&
+            parsed.maxHeartRate != null &&
+            parsed.maxHeartRate < parsed.avgHeartRate
+        ) {
+            out[CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE] = "max heart rate is below average"
+        }
+        intError(
+            text(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE),
+            parsed.cadence,
+            cadenceMin(activity),
+            cadenceMax(activity),
+            cadenceUnit(activity),
+        )?.let { out[CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE] = it }
+        intError(
+            text(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER),
+            parsed.power,
+            MIN_POWER,
+            MAX_POWER,
+            "W",
+        )?.let { out[CardioLogField.AVG_POWER] = it }
+        intError(
+            text(CardioLogField.CALORIES),
+            parsed.calories,
+            0,
+            MAX_CALORIES,
+            "kcal",
+        )?.let { out[CardioLogField.CALORIES] = it }
+        effortError(text(CardioLogField.EFFORT), effort)?.let { out[CardioLogField.EFFORT] = it }
+        return out
+    }
+
+    /** True when Duration parses and nothing is in error. */
+    val canSubmit: Boolean
+        get() = parsed().durationSeconds != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    /**
+     * The calculated pace or speed, shown while its field stays blank — "6:37", "31.9" — null when
+     * typed and for every other field.
+     */
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        val parsed = parsed()
+        return when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> {
+                if (text(field).isBlank()) {
+                    CardioFormat
+                        .derivedPace(
+                            parsed.durationSeconds,
+                            parsed.distance,
+                            parsed.paceSeconds,
+                            activity,
+                            units,
+                        )?.let { formatDuration(it) }
+                } else {
+                    null
+                }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> {
+                if (text(field).isBlank()) {
+                    CardioFormat
+                        .derivedSpeed(
+                            parsed.durationSeconds,
+                            parsed.distance,
+                            parsed.speed,
+                            units,
+                        )?.let { formatSpeed(it) }
+                } else {
+                    null
+                }
+            }
+
+            else -> {
+                null
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** Typed fields and effort; never the note, never a derived value. */
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest {
+        val parsed = parsed()
+        return CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = parsed.durationSeconds ?: 0,
+            distance = parsed.distance,
+            avgPaceSeconds = parsed.paceSeconds,
+            avgSpeed = parsed.speed,
+            avgHeartRate = parsed.avgHeartRate,
+            maxHeartRate = parsed.maxHeartRate,
+            calories = parsed.calories,
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = parsed.elevation,
+            avgCadence = parsed.cadence,
+            avgPower = parsed.power,
+        )
+    }
+
+    private fun parsed(): Parsed = parse(texts, activity, units)
+
+    companion object {
+        /**
+         * A blank draft; Duration comes from the clock when the workout is in progress, floored to
+         * the second.
+         */
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val draft = CardioLogDraft(activity = workout.activity, units = workout.distanceUnit)
+            val startedAt = workout.startedAt
+            if (workout.status != WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS || startedAt == null) return draft
+            val seconds = (now - startedAt).inWholeSeconds
+            if (seconds < 0) return draft
+            return draft.with(CardioLogField.DURATION, formatDuration(seconds.toInt()))
+        }
+
+        /** The saved log back as text, effort from it, note from the workout's notes. */
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val log = workout.log ?: return CardioLogDraft(activity = workout.activity, units = workout.distanceUnit)
+            var draft =
+                CardioLogDraft(
+                    activity = workout.activity,
+                    units = workout.distanceUnit,
+                    effort = log.effort,
+                    note = workout.notes ?: "",
+                )
+            draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.DURATION, formatDuration(log.durationSeconds))
+            log.distance?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, formatDistance(it, workout)) }
+            log.avgPaceSeconds?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, formatDuration(it)) }
+            log.avgSpeed?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED, formatSpeed(it)) }
+            log.elevationGain?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN, it.toString()) }
+            log.avgHeartRate?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, it.toString()) }
+            log.maxHeartRate?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, it.toString()) }
+            log.avgCadence?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, it.toString()) }
+            log.avgPower?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER, it.toString()) }
+            log.calories?.let { draft = draft.with(CardioLogField.CALORIES, it.toString()) }
+            if (draft.effort != null) {
+                draft = draft.copy(texts = draft.texts + (CardioLogField.EFFORT to draft.effort.toString()))
+            }
+            return draft
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+private data class Parsed(
+    val durationSeconds: Int?,
+    val distance: CardioDistance?,
+    val paceSeconds: Int?,
+    val speed: Double?,
+    val elevation: Int?,
+    val avgHeartRate: Int?,
+    val maxHeartRate: Int?,
+    val cadence: Int?,
+    val power: Int?,
+    val calories: Int?,
+)
+
+private fun parse(
+    texts: Map<CardioLogField, String>,
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+): Parsed {
+    val unit = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else units
+    return Parsed(
+        durationSeconds = parseClock(texts[CardioLogField.DURATION] ?: "", allowHours = true),
+        distance = (texts[CardioLogField.DISTANCE] ?: "").toDoubleOrNull()?.let { CardioDistance(it, unit) },
+        paceSeconds = parseClock(texts[CardioLogField.AVG_PACE] ?: "", allowHours = false),
+        speed = (texts[CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED] ?: "").toDoubleOrNull(),
+        elevation = (texts[CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN] ?: "").toIntOrNull(),
+        avgHeartRate = (texts[CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE] ?: "").toIntOrNull(),
+        maxHeartRate = (texts[CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE] ?: "").toIntOrNull(),
+        cadence = (texts[CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE] ?: "").toIntOrNull(),
+        power = (texts[CardioLogField.AVG_POWER] ?: "").toIntOrNull(),
+        calories = (texts[CardioLogField.CALORIES] ?: "").toIntOrNull(),
+    )
+}
+
+private fun cadenceMin(activity: CardioActivity): Int = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) MIN_ROWER_CADENCE else MIN_CADENCE
+
+private fun cadenceMax(activity: CardioActivity): Int = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) MAX_ROWER_CADENCE else MAX_CADENCE
+
+private fun cadenceUnit(activity: CardioActivity): String = if (activity.isRide) "rpm" else "spm"
+
+private fun elevationUnit(units: DistanceUnit): String = if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "ft" else "m"
+
+private fun distanceUnit(
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+): String =
+    when {
+        activity == CardioActivity.ROWER -> "m"
+        units == DistanceUnit.MI -> "mi"
+        else -> "km"
+    }
+
+private fun distanceMax(
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+): Double =
+    when {
+        activity == CardioActivity.ROWER -> MAX_DISTANCE_KM * DistanceUnit.KM.metres
+        units == DistanceUnit.MI -> MAX_DISTANCE_KM * DistanceUnit.KM.metres / DistanceUnit.MI.metres
+        else -> MAX_DISTANCE_KM
+    }
+
+private fun paceUnit(
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+): String = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) "/500 m" else "/${units.name.lowercase()}"
+
+private fun speedUnit(units: DistanceUnit): String = if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "mph" else "km/h"
+
+private fun effortError(
+    raw: String,
+    effort: Int?,
+): String? {
+    if (raw.isBlank()) return null
+    return if (effort == null || effort !in MIN_EFFORT..MAX_EFFORT) "between $MIN_EFFORT and $MAX_EFFORT" else null
+}
+
+private fun durationError(
+    raw: String,
+    seconds: Int?,
+): String? {
+    if (raw.isBlank()) return null
+    if (seconds == null) return "use h:mm:ss"
+    return if (seconds !in 1..MAX_DURATION_SECONDS) "between 1 and $MAX_DURATION_SECONDS s" else null
+}
+
+private fun distanceError(
+    raw: String,
+    distance: CardioDistance?,
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+): String? {
+    if (raw.isBlank()) return null
+    val limit = MAX_DISTANCE_KM * DistanceUnit.KM.metres
+    val metres = distance?.let { it.value * it.unit.metres }
+    return if (metres == null || metres <= 0 || metres >= limit) {
+        rangeMessage(0.0, distanceMax(activity, units), distanceUnit(activity, units))
+    } else {
+        null
+    }
+}
+
+private fun paceError(
+    raw: String,
+    seconds: Int?,
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+): String? {
+    if (raw.isBlank()) return null
+    if (seconds == null) return "use h:mm:ss"
+    val max = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) MAX_ROWER_PACE_SECONDS else MAX_PACE_SECONDS
+    return if (seconds !in MIN_PACE_SECONDS..max) {
+        "between ${formatDuration(MIN_PACE_SECONDS)} and ${formatDuration(max)} ${paceUnit(activity, units)}"
+    } else {
+        null
+    }
+}
+
+private fun speedError(
+    raw: String,
+    speed: Double?,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+): String? {
+    if (raw.isBlank()) return null
+    return if (speed == null || speed !in MIN_SPEED..MAX_SPEED) {
+        rangeMessage(MIN_SPEED, MAX_SPEED, speedUnit(units))
+    } else {
+        null
+    }
+}
+
+/** `m:ss` or `h:mm:ss`; minutes and seconds after a colon are 00–59, whole seconds. */
+private fun parseClock(
+    raw: String,
+    allowHours: Boolean,
+): Int? {
+    if (raw.isBlank()) return null
+    val parts = raw.split(":")
+    if (parts.any { it.isEmpty() || it.any { char -> !char.isDigit() } }) return null
+    return when (parts.size) {
+        2 -> parseMinutesSeconds(parts)
+        3 -> if (allowHours) parseHoursMinutesSeconds(parts) else null
+        else -> null
+    }
+}
+
+private fun parseMinutesSeconds(parts: List<String>): Int? {
+    val minutes = parts[0].toIntOrNull()
+    val seconds = parts[1].toIntOrNull()
+    return if (minutes == null || seconds == null || parts[1].length > 2 || seconds >= SECONDS_PER_MINUTE) {
+        null
+    } else {
+        minutes * SECONDS_PER_MINUTE + seconds
+    }
+}
+
+private fun parseHoursMinutesSeconds(parts: List<String>): Int? {
+    val hours = parts[0].toIntOrNull()
+    val minutes = parts[1].toIntOrNull()
+    val seconds = parts[2].toIntOrNull()
+    val longTail = parts[1].length > 2 || parts[2].length > 2
+    return if (hours == null || minutes == null || seconds == null || longTail) {
+        null
+    } else if (minutes >= SECONDS_PER_MINUTE || seconds >= SECONDS_PER_MINUTE) {
+        null
+    } else {
+        hours * SECONDS_PER_HOUR + minutes * SECONDS_PER_MINUTE + seconds
+    }
+}
+
+private fun intError(
+    raw: String,
+    value: Int?,
+    min: Int,
+    max: Int,
+    unit: String,
+): String? {
+    if (raw.isBlank()) return null
+    if (value == null || value !in min..max) return "between $min and $max $unit"
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun rangeMessage(
+    min: Double,
+    max: Double,
+    unit: String,
+): String = "between ${formatNumber(min)} and ${formatNumber(max)} $unit"
+
+private fun formatNumber(value: Double): String =
+    if (value == value.roundToLong().toDouble()) {
+        value.roundToLong().toString()
+    } else {
+        ((value * 100).roundToInt() / 100.0).toString()
+    }
+
+/** `m:ss` under an hour, `h:mm:ss` from one up. */
+private fun formatDuration(seconds: Int): String = if (seconds < SECONDS_PER_HOUR) clock(seconds) else hourClock(seconds)
+
+private fun formatSpeed(value: Double): String = ((value * TEN).roundToLong() / TEN).toString()
+
+private fun formatDistance(
+    distance: CardioDistance,
+    workout: CardioWorkout,
+): String =
+    if (workout.activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) {
+        (distance.value * distance.unit.metres).roundToLong().toString()
+    } else {
+        formatNumber(distance.value)
+    }

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+/**
+ * One row of the log sheet, in sheet order (KAN-939 `log-fields-table`); the sets mirror the
+ * backend's `CardioActivity.logFields`.
+ */
+enum class CardioLogField {
+    DURATION,
+    DISTANCE,
+    AVG_PACE,
+    AVG_SPEED,
+    ELEVATION_GAIN,
+    AVG_HEART_RATE,
+    MAX_HEART_RATE,
+    AVG_CADENCE,
+    AVG_POWER,
+    CALORIES,
+    EFFORT,
+    NOTE,
+}
+
+/** This activity's log sheet rows, in the order the sheet draws them. */
+val CardioActivity.logFields: List<CardioLogField>
+    get() =
+        when (this) {
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN -> {
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                    CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                    CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                    CardioLogField.NOTE,
+                )
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE -> {
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED,
+                    CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                    CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                    CardioLogField.NOTE,
+                )
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE -> {
+                CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields - CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.ROWER -> {
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                    CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                    CardioLogField.NOTE,
+                )
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN -> {
+                CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN.logFields -
+                    setOf(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN, CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE)
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL -> {
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                    CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                    CardioLogField.NOTE,
+                )
+            }
+        }

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlinx.datetime.LocalDate
+import kotlin.test.Test
+import kotlin.test.assertEquals
+import kotlin.test.assertFalse
+import kotlin.test.assertNull
+import kotlin.test.assertTrue
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+class CardioLogDraftTest {
+    private fun workout(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        units: DistanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+        status: WorkoutStatus = WorkoutStatus.PLANNED,
+        startedAt: Instant? = null,
+        log: CardioLog? = null,
+        notes: String? = null,
+    ): CardioWorkout =
+        CardioWorkout(
+            id = "w1",
+            scheduledDate = LocalDate(2026, 5, 4),
+            displayName = "Run",
+            notes = notes,
+            status = status,
+            startedAt = startedAt,
+            activity = activity,
+            kind = activity.kinds.first(),
+            distanceUnit = units,
+            log = log,
+        )
+
+    private fun typed(vararg pairs: Pair<CardioLogField, String>): CardioLogDraft =
+        typed(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN, DistanceUnit.KM, *pairs)
+
+    private fun typed(
+        activity: CardioActivity,
+        units: DistanceUnit,
+        vararg pairs: Pair<CardioLogField, String>,
+    ): CardioLogDraft {
+        var draft = CardioLogDraft(activity = activity, units = units)
+        pairs.forEach { (field, value) -> draft = draft.with(field, value) }
+        return draft
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationReadsHoursMinutesAndSeconds() {
+        val draft =
+            typed(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION to "33:12",
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE to "5.02",
+            )
+        assertEquals(1992, draft.toRequest().durationSeconds)
+        val hours =
+            typed(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION to "1:12:40",
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE to "38.6",
+            )
+        assertEquals(4360, hours.toRequest().durationSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationRefusesOtherShapes() {
+        listOf("45", "1:75", "abc").forEach { raw ->
+            val draft = typed(CardioLogField.DURATION to raw).left(CardioLogField.DURATION)
+            assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", draft.shownError(CardioLogField.DURATION), raw)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun paceIsDerivedWhenBlankAndNeverSent() {
+        val draft =
+            typed(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION to "33:12",
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE to "5.02",
+            )
+        assertEquals("6:37", draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertNull(draft.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun speedIsDerivedForRides() {
+        val draft =
+            typed(
+                CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE,
+                DistanceUnit.KM,
+                CardioLogField.DURATION to "1:12:40",
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE to "38.6",
+            )
+        assertEquals("31.9", draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRowerDerivesPerFiveHundredMetres() {
+        val draft =
+            typed(
+                CardioActivity.ROWER,
+                DistanceUnit.KM,
+                CardioLogField.DURATION to "29:54",
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE to "7120",
+            )
+        assertEquals("2:06", draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(7120.0, DistanceUnit.M), draft.toRequest().distance)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun maxBelowAvgIsAnError() {
+        val draft =
+            typed(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION to "33:12",
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE to "148",
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE to "139",
+            )
+        assertEquals("max heart rate is below average", draft.errors()[CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE])
+        assertNull(draft.errors()[CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowerCadenceOutOfRangeNamesTheRange() {
+        val draft =
+            typed(
+                CardioActivity.ROWER,
+                DistanceUnit.KM,
+                CardioLogField.DURATION to "29:54",
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE to "8",
+            )
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", draft.errors()[CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anErrorShowsOnlyOnceTheFieldWasLeft() {
+        val draft = typed(CardioLogField.DURATION to "abc")
+        assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", draft.errors()[CardioLogField.DURATION])
+        assertNull(draft.shownError(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+        assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", draft.left(CardioLogField.DURATION).shownError(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationPrefillsFromTheClockRoundedDown() {
+        val draft =
+            CardioLogDraft.fresh(
+                workout(
+                    status = WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS,
+                    startedAt = Instant.parse("2026-05-04T18:31:00Z"),
+                ),
+                Instant.parse("2026-05-04T19:12:06.900Z"),
+            )
+        assertEquals("41:06", draft.text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aSavedLogFillsTheDraft() {
+        val draft =
+            CardioLogDraft.of(
+                workout(
+                    log =
+                        CardioLog(
+                            durationSeconds = 1992,
+                            distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                            avgPaceSeconds = 397,
+                            avgHeartRate = 148,
+                            maxHeartRate = 159,
+                            calories = 320,
+                            effort = 6,
+                            elevationGain = 42,
+                            avgCadence = 178,
+                        ),
+                    notes = "hilly",
+                ),
+            )
+        assertEquals("33:12", draft.text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+        assertEquals("5.02", draft.text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE))
+        assertEquals("6:37", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertNull(draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("148", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("159", draft.text(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("320", draft.text(CardioLogField.CALORIES))
+        assertEquals("42", draft.text(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN))
+        assertEquals("178", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals(6, draft.copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly").effort)
+        assertEquals("hilly", draft.note)
+        assertTrue(draft.errors().isEmpty())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRequestCarriesTypedFieldsOnly() {
+        val draft =
+            typed(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION to "33:12",
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE to "5.02",
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE to "148",
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES to "320",
+            ).copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly")
+        val request = draft.toRequest()
+        assertEquals(1992, request.durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM), request.distance)
+        assertEquals(148, request.avgHeartRate)
+        assertEquals(320, request.calories)
+        assertEquals(6, request.effort)
+        assertNull(request.avgPaceSeconds)
+        assertNull(request.avgSpeed)
+        assertNull(request.maxHeartRate)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun logFieldsFollowSheetOrder() {
+        val run = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN.logFields
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            run,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED,
+                CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields - CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+            CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.ROWER.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            run - setOf(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN, CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE),
+            CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL.logFields,
+        )
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun canSubmitNeedsADurationAndNoErrors() {
+        assertFalse(CardioLogDraft().canSubmit)
+        assertTrue(typed(CardioLogField.DURATION to "33:12").canSubmit)
+        assertFalse(typed(CardioLogField.DURATION to "abc").canSubmit)
+        assertFalse(
+            typed(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION to "33:12",
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE to "148",
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE to "139",
+            ).canSubmit,
+        )
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun savedDerivedPaceRecomputes() {
+        val draft =
+            CardioLogDraft.of(
+                workout(
+                    log =
+                        CardioLog(
+                            durationSeconds = 4360,
+                            distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                            derivedAvgPaceSeconds = 869,
+                        ),
+                ),
+            )
+        assertEquals("", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("14:29", draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+    }
+}

Done. The log logic is implemented, green, and committed as [79f6c99](https://example.com/commit/79f6c998227a76151dcd35c4fdef69eeacc45551) (3 files, 704 insertions).

What changed:
- [CardioLogFields.kt](/work/shared/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/gymie/shared/cardio/CardioLogFields.kt): the `CardioLogField` enum and `CardioActivity.logFields` in sheet order — outdoor run L1, outdoor ride L3, indoor ride (ride minus elevation), rower L4, treadmill (run minus elevation and cadence), elliptical. The backend `BC/core/domain/cardio/CardioActivity.kt` was not present in this checkout, so the table follows the task's stated sets exactly.
- [CardioLogDraft.kt](/work/shared/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/gymie/shared/cardio/CardioLogDraft.kt): immutable data class with `effort: Int?` and `note: String` copy-compatible properties, plus `texts` and `left`. Duration accepts `m:ss`/`h:mm:ss` (anything else is `use h:mm:ss`); pace accepts `m:ss`; ranges follow the spec (duration 1–86400 s, HR 30–250 bpm with `max heart rate is below average` on max only, rower cadence 10–60, all messages `between X and Y <unit>`); rower distance is metres in both directions; `derived()` formats via `CardioFormat.derivedPace`/`derivedSpeed` (`6:37`, `31.9`, `2:06` per 500 m) and is null when typed; `toRequest()` carries typed fields plus effort, never note or derived values; `fresh()` floors `now − startedAt` to the second (`41:06`); `of()` restores typed values as text while leaving derived pace/speed blank so they recompute (`14:29` for 4360 s over 5.02 km).
- [CardioLogDraftTest.kt](/work/shared/src/desktopTest/kotlin/com/gymie/shared/cardio/CardioLogDraftTest.kt): 14 tests covering the eleven named assertion families plus field-table order, effort/note `copy()`, and `canSubmit` gating.

Commands and results:
- `./gradlew :shared:desktopTest --tests '*CardioLogDraftTest'` — 14 tests, 0 failures (confirmed in the test-results XML).
- `./gradlew :shared:ktlintFormat`, then `:shared:ktlintCheck :shared:detekt :shared:desktopTest --tests 'CardioLogDraftTest'` — BUILD SUCCESSFUL. Detekt initially flagged `errors()` complexity and too many class functions; both were fixed by splitting per-field checks into file-private functions. +package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlin.math.roundToLong
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+private const val SECONDS_PER_MINUTE = 60
+private const val SECONDS_PER_HOUR = 3600
+private const val MAX_CLOCK_PART = 59
+private const val MAX_DURATION_SECONDS = 86400
+private const val MIN_HEART_RATE = 30
+private const val MAX_HEART_RATE = 250
+private const val MIN_CALORIES = 0
+private const val MAX_CALORIES = 20000
+private const val MIN_ELEVATION = 0
+private const val MAX_ELEVATION = 20000
+private const val MIN_PACE_SECONDS = 60
+private const val MAX_PACE_SECONDS = 3600
+private const val MAX_ROWER_PACE_SECONDS = 600
+private const val MIN_SPEED = 0.1
+private const val MAX_SPEED = 120.0
+private const val MIN_CADENCE = 1
+private const val MAX_CADENCE = 250
+private const val MIN_ROWER_CADENCE = 10
+private const val MAX_ROWER_CADENCE = 60
+private const val MIN_POWER = 1
+private const val MAX_POWER = 2500
+private const val MAX_DISTANCE_METRES = 1000000.0
+
+/**
+ * The log sheet's working copy (KAN-939 step 4): an immutable data class with pure functions, no
+ * Compose. [texts] holds what is typed per field, [effort] and [note] are properties so
+ * `copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly")` works, and [left] marks fields the user has left — range
+ * errors surface through [shownError] only once left (the 400 ms debounce lives in the sheet).
+ * A blank pace/speed with both duration and distance is derived for display ([derived]) and never
+ * sent ([toRequest] carries typed fields only, never the note).
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity,
+    val units: DistanceUnit,
+    val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val effort: Int? = null,
+    val note: String = "",
+    val left: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+) {
+    /** What the field shows; "" when nothing is typed. */
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> note
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> effort?.toString() ?: ""
+            else -> texts[field] ?: ""
+        }
+
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> copy(note = value)
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> copy(effort = value.trim().toIntOrNull())
+            else -> copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+        }
+
+    /** Marks the field as left, so its error may show. */
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(left = left + field)
+
+    /** [errors]' entry once the field was left, else null. */
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (field in left) errors()[field] else null
+
+    /** Every non-blank field outside its range; an empty field is never in error. */
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> {
+        val found = mutableMapOf<CardioLogField, String>()
+        for (field in CardioLogField.entries) {
+            val message = checkField(field) ?: continue
+            found[field] = message
+        }
+        return found
+    }
+
+    /** Duration parses and nothing is in error. */
+    val canSubmit: Boolean
+        get() = parsedDuration() != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    /**
+     * The calculated display for a blank pace/speed ("6:37", "31.9"); null when typed — and for
+     * every other field.
+     */
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        if (field == CardioLogField.AVG_PACE) {
+            val pace =
+                CardioFormat.derivedPace(parsedDuration(), parsedDistance(), parsedPace(), activity, units)
+            if (pace == null) return null
+            return paceText(pace)
+        }
+        if (field == CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED) {
+            val speed = CardioFormat.derivedSpeed(parsedDuration(), parsedDistance(), parsedSpeed(), units)
+            if (speed == null) return null
+            return speedText(speed)
+        }
+        return null
+    }
+
+    /** Typed fields and effort; never the note, never a derived value. */
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest =
+        CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = parsedDuration() ?: 0,
+            distance = parsedDistance(),
+            avgPaceSeconds = parsedPace(),
+            avgSpeed = parsedSpeed(),
+            avgHeartRate = intOrNull(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE),
+            maxHeartRate = intOrNull(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE),
+            calories = intOrNull(CardioLogField.CALORIES),
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = intOrNull(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN),
+            avgCadence = intOrNull(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE),
+            avgPower = intOrNull(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER),
+        )
+
+    companion object {
+        /** A blank draft; duration prefills from the clock when in progress, floored to the second. */
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val startedAt = workout.startedAt
+            val duration =
+                if (workout.status == WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS && startedAt != null) {
+                    val elapsed = (now - startedAt).inWholeSeconds
+                    if (elapsed > 0) durationText(elapsed.toInt()) else ""
+                } else {
+                    ""
+                }
+            val texts =
+                if (duration.isEmpty()) {
+                    emptyMap()
+                } else {
+                    mapOf(CardioLogField.DURATION to duration)
+                }
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                units = workout.distanceUnit,
+                texts = texts,
+                note = workout.notes.orEmpty(),
+            )
+        }
+
+        /** The saved log back as text with effort from it and the note from the workout's notes. */
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val log = workout.log
+            val texts = mutableMapOf<CardioLogField, String>()
+            if (log != null) {
+                texts[CardioLogField.DURATION] = durationText(log.durationSeconds)
+                log.distance?.let { texts[CardioLogField.DISTANCE] = distanceText(it, workout) }
+                log.avgPaceSeconds?.let { texts[CardioLogField.AVG_PACE] = paceText(it) }
+                log.avgSpeed?.let { texts[CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED] = speedText(it) }
+                log.elevationGain?.let { texts[CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN] = it.toString() }
+                log.avgHeartRate?.let { texts[CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE] = it.toString() }
+                log.maxHeartRate?.let { texts[CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE] = it.toString() }
+                log.avgCadence?.let { texts[CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE] = it.toString() }
+                log.avgPower?.let { texts[CardioLogField.AVG_POWER] = it.toString() }
+                log.calories?.let { texts[CardioLogField.CALORIES] = it.toString() }
+            }
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                units = workout.distanceUnit,
+                texts = texts,
+                effort = log?.effort,
+                note = workout.notes.orEmpty(),
+            )
+        }
+
+        private fun distanceText(
+            distance: CardioDistance,
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+        ): String {
+            val metres = distance.value * distance.unit.metres
+            if (workout.activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) return metres.roundToLong().toString()
+            val value = metres / workout.distanceUnit.metres
+            return if (value % 1.0 == 0.0) value.toLong().toString() else value.toString()
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.checkField(field: CardioLogField): String? =
+    when (field) {
+        CardioLogField.DURATION -> durationCheck()
+        CardioLogField.DISTANCE -> distanceCheck()
+        CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> paceCheck()
+        CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> speedCheck()
+        CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN -> elevationCheck()
+        CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE -> avgHeartRateCheck()
+        CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE -> maxHeartRateCheck()
+        CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE -> cadenceCheck()
+        CardioLogField.AVG_POWER -> powerCheck()
+        CardioLogField.CALORIES -> caloriesCheck()
+        CardioLogField.EFFORT, CardioLogField.NOTE -> null
+    }
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.durationCheck(): String? {
+    val durationText = text(CardioLogField.DURATION)
+    if (durationText.isBlank()) return null
+    val seconds = parseDuration(durationText) ?: return "use h:mm:ss"
+    if (seconds !in 1..MAX_DURATION_SECONDS) return "between 1 and $MAX_DURATION_SECONDS s"
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.distanceCheck(): String? {
+    val distanceText = text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE)
+    if (distanceText.isBlank()) return null
+    val metres = distanceText.trim().toDoubleOrNull()?.let { it * distanceUnit().metres }
+    if (metres == null || metres <= 0 || metres >= MAX_DISTANCE_METRES) {
+        val max = MAX_DISTANCE_METRES / distanceUnit().metres
+        val maxText = if (max % 1.0 == 0.0) max.toLong().toString() else max.toString()
+        return "between 0 and $maxText ${distanceUnit().name.lowercase()}"
+    }
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.paceCheck(): String? {
+    val paceText = text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE)
+    if (paceText.isBlank()) return null
+    val pace = parsePace(paceText) ?: return "use m:ss"
+    val maxPace = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) MAX_ROWER_PACE_SECONDS else MAX_PACE_SECONDS
+    if (pace !in MIN_PACE_SECONDS..maxPace) {
+        val unit = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) "/500 m" else "/${units.name.lowercase()}"
+        return "between ${paceText(MIN_PACE_SECONDS)} and ${paceText(maxPace)} $unit"
+    }
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.speedCheck(): String? {
+    val speedText = text(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED)
+    if (speedText.isBlank()) return null
+    val speed = speedText.trim().toDoubleOrNull()
+    if (speed == null || speed < MIN_SPEED || speed > MAX_SPEED) {
+        val maxText = if (MAX_SPEED % 1.0 == 0.0) MAX_SPEED.toLong().toString() else MAX_SPEED.toString()
+        val unit = if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "mph" else "km/h"
+        return "between $MIN_SPEED and $maxText $unit"
+    }
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.elevationCheck(): String? {
+    val elevationText = text(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN)
+    if (elevationText.isBlank()) return null
+    val elevation = elevationText.trim().toIntOrNull()
+    if (elevation == null || elevation !in MIN_ELEVATION..MAX_ELEVATION) {
+        val unit = if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "ft" else "m"
+        return "between $MIN_ELEVATION and $MAX_ELEVATION $unit"
+    }
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.avgHeartRateCheck(): String? {
+    if (text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE).isBlank()) return null
+    val avgHr = intOrNull(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE)
+    if (avgHr == null || avgHr !in MIN_HEART_RATE..MAX_HEART_RATE) {
+        return "between $MIN_HEART_RATE and $MAX_HEART_RATE bpm"
+    }
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.maxHeartRateCheck(): String? {
+    if (text(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE).isBlank()) return null
+    val maxHr = intOrNull(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE)
+    if (maxHr == null || maxHr !in MIN_HEART_RATE..MAX_HEART_RATE) {
+        return "between $MIN_HEART_RATE and $MAX_HEART_RATE bpm"
+    }
+    val avgHr = intOrNull(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE)
+    if (avgHr != null && maxHr < avgHr) return "max heart rate is below average"
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.cadenceCheck(): String? {
+    if (text(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE).isBlank()) return null
+    val cadence = intOrNull(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE)
+    val min = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) MIN_ROWER_CADENCE else MIN_CADENCE
+    val max = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) MAX_ROWER_CADENCE else MAX_CADENCE
+    if (cadence == null || cadence !in min..max) {
+        val unit = if (activity.isRide) "rpm" else "spm"
+        return "between $min and $max $unit"
+    }
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.powerCheck(): String? {
+    if (text(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER).isBlank()) return null
+    val power = intOrNull(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER)
+    if (power == null || power !in MIN_POWER..MAX_POWER) return "between $MIN_POWER and $MAX_POWER W"
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.caloriesCheck(): String? {
+    if (text(CardioLogField.CALORIES).isBlank()) return null
+    val calories = intOrNull(CardioLogField.CALORIES)
+    if (calories == null || calories !in MIN_CALORIES..MAX_CALORIES) {
+        return "between $MIN_CALORIES and $MAX_CALORIES kcal"
+    }
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.parsedDuration(): Int? {
+    val durationText = text(CardioLogField.DURATION)
+    return if (durationText.isBlank()) null else parseDuration(durationText)
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.parsedPace(): Int? {
+    val paceText = text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE)
+    return if (paceText.isBlank()) null else parsePace(paceText)
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.parsedSpeed(): Double? {
+    val speedText = text(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED)
+    return if (speedText.isBlank()) null else speedText.trim().toDoubleOrNull()
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.parsedDistance(): CardioDistance? {
+    val distanceText = text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE)
+    if (distanceText.isBlank()) return null
+    val value = distanceText.trim().toDoubleOrNull() ?: return null
+    return CardioDistance(value, distanceUnit())
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.intOrNull(field: CardioLogField): Int? {
+    val value = text(field)
+    return if (value.isBlank()) null else value.trim().toIntOrNull()
+}
+
+/** The rower's distance is metres whatever the workout's unit. */
+private fun CardioLogDraft.distanceUnit(): DistanceUnit {
+    if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) return DistanceUnit.M
+    return units
+}
+
+/** Whole seconds from `m:ss` or `h:mm:ss`; minutes and seconds after a colon run 00–59. */
+private fun parseDuration(text: String): Int? {
+    val parts = text.trim().split(":")
+    if (parts.size != 2 && parts.size != 3) return null
+    val head = parts[0].takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() && it.all(Char::isDigit) }?.toIntOrNull() ?: return null
+    val tail =
+        parts.drop(1).map { part ->
+            if (part.isEmpty() || part.length > 2 || !part.all(Char::isDigit)) return null
+            part.toInt().takeIf { it in 0..MAX_CLOCK_PART } ?: return null
+        }
+    return if (parts.size == 2) {
+        head * SECONDS_PER_MINUTE + tail[0]
+    } else {
+        head * SECONDS_PER_HOUR + tail[0] * SECONDS_PER_MINUTE + tail[1]
+    }
+}
+
+/** Whole seconds from `m:ss`. */
+private fun parsePace(text: String): Int? {
+    val parts = text.trim().split(":")
+    if (parts.size != 2) return null
+    if (parts[0].isEmpty() || !parts[0].all(Char::isDigit)) return null
+    val seconds = parts[1]
+    if (seconds.isEmpty() || seconds.length > 2 || !seconds.all(Char::isDigit)) return null
+    if (seconds.toInt() !in 0..MAX_CLOCK_PART) return null
+    return parts[0].toInt() * SECONDS_PER_MINUTE + seconds.toInt()
+}
+
+/** `m:ss` under an hour, `h:mm:ss` from one up (`41:06`, `1:12:40`). */
+private fun durationText(totalSeconds: Int): String {
+    val hours = totalSeconds / SECONDS_PER_HOUR
+    val minutes = (totalSeconds % SECONDS_PER_HOUR) / SECONDS_PER_MINUTE
+    val seconds = totalSeconds % SECONDS_PER_MINUTE
+    return if (hours == 0) {
+        "$minutes:${seconds.toString().padStart(2, '0')}"
+    } else {
+        "$hours:${minutes.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${seconds.toString().padStart(2, '0')}"
+    }
+}
+
+/** Pace as `m:ss`, always (`60:00` at the top of the range, never `1:00:00`). */
+private fun paceText(totalSeconds: Int): String =
+    "${totalSeconds / SECONDS_PER_MINUTE}:${(totalSeconds % SECONDS_PER_MINUTE).toString().padStart(2, '0')}"
+
+/** Speed to one decimal (`31.9`). */
+private fun speedText(value: Double): String {
+    val rounded = (value * 10).roundToLong()
+    return "${rounded / 10}.${rounded % 10}"
+}

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+/**
+ * One row of the log sheet (KAN-939 step 4, decision `log-fields-table`); the sheet order is the
+ * frames' (L1, L3, L4) and step-4's prose for the undrawn three. Every activity takes duration, the
+ * heart rates, calories, effort and a note, plus its own measures — mirroring the backend's
+ * `CardioActivity.logFields` sets.
+ */
+enum class CardioLogField {
+    DURATION,
+    DISTANCE,
+    AVG_PACE,
+    AVG_SPEED,
+    ELEVATION_GAIN,
+    AVG_HEART_RATE,
+    MAX_HEART_RATE,
+    AVG_CADENCE,
+    AVG_POWER,
+    CALORIES,
+    EFFORT,
+    NOTE,
+}
+
+/** [CardioActivity]'s log rows in sheet order. */
+val CardioActivity.logFields: List<CardioLogField>
+    get() =
+        when (this) {
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN -> {
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                    CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                    CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                    CardioLogField.NOTE,
+                )
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE -> {
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED,
+                    CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                    CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                    CardioLogField.NOTE,
+                )
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE -> {
+                CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields - CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.ROWER -> {
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                    CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                    CardioLogField.NOTE,
+                )
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN -> {
+                CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN.logFields -
+                    setOf(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN, CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE)
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL -> {
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                    CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                    CardioLogField.NOTE,
+                )
+            }
+        }

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlinx.datetime.LocalDate
+import kotlin.test.Test
+import kotlin.test.assertEquals
+import kotlin.test.assertFalse
+import kotlin.test.assertNull
+import kotlin.test.assertTrue
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+class CardioLogDraftTest {
+    private fun workout(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        units: DistanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+        status: WorkoutStatus = WorkoutStatus.PLANNED,
+        startedAt: Instant? = null,
+        log: CardioLog? = null,
+        notes: String? = null,
+    ) = CardioWorkout(
+        id = "w1",
+        scheduledDate = LocalDate(2026, 10, 27),
+        displayName = "Run",
+        notes = notes,
+        status = status,
+        startedAt = startedAt,
+        activity = activity,
+        kind = activity.kinds.first(),
+        distanceUnit = units,
+        log = log,
+    )
+
+    private fun blank(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        units: DistanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+    ) = CardioLogDraft(activity = activity, units = units)
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationReadsHoursMinutesAndSeconds() {
+        assertEquals(1992, blank().with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(4360, blank().with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "1:12:40").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationRefusesOtherShapes() {
+        listOf("45", "1:75", "abc").forEach { shape ->
+            assertEquals(
+                "use h:mm:ss",
+                blank().with(CardioLogField.DURATION, shape).errors()[CardioLogField.DURATION],
+                shape,
+            )
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun paceIsDerivedWhenBlankAndNeverSent() {
+        val draft =
+            blank()
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "5.02")
+        assertEquals("6:37", draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertNull(draft.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun speedIsDerivedForRides() {
+        val draft =
+            blank(activity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "1:12:40")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "38.6")
+        assertEquals("31.9", draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED))
+        assertNull(draft.toRequest().avgSpeed)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRowerDerivesPerFiveHundredMetres() {
+        val draft =
+            blank(activity = CardioActivity.ROWER)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "29:54")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "7120")
+        assertEquals("2:06", draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun maxBelowAvgIsAnError() {
+        val draft =
+            blank()
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, "148")
+                .with(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, "139")
+        assertEquals(
+            "max heart rate is below average",
+            draft.errors()[CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE],
+        )
+        assertNull(draft.errors()[CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowerCadenceOutOfRangeNamesTheRange() {
+        val draft =
+            blank(activity = CardioActivity.ROWER)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "29:54")
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, "8")
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", draft.errors()[CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anErrorShowsOnlyOnceTheFieldWasLeft() {
+        val field = CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+        val draft =
+            blank(activity = CardioActivity.ROWER)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "29:54")
+                .with(field, "8")
+        assertTrue(draft.errors().containsKey(field))
+        assertNull(draft.shownError(field))
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", draft.left(field).shownError(field))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationPrefillsFromTheClockRoundedDown() {
+        val draft =
+            CardioLogDraft.fresh(
+                workout(
+                    status = WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS,
+                    startedAt = Instant.parse("2026-10-27T18:31:00Z"),
+                ),
+                Instant.parse("2026-10-27T19:12:06.900Z"),
+            )
+        assertEquals("41:06", draft.text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aSavedLogFillsTheDraft() {
+        val draft =
+            CardioLogDraft.of(
+                workout(
+                    status = WorkoutStatus.FINISHED,
+                    notes = "hilly",
+                    log =
+                        CardioLog(
+                            durationSeconds = 4360,
+                            distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                            avgHeartRate = 148,
+                            maxHeartRate = 156,
+                            calories = 520,
+                            effort = 7,
+                            elevationGain = 120,
+                            avgCadence = 178,
+                            derivedAvgPaceSeconds = 869,
+                        ),
+                ),
+            )
+        assertEquals("1:12:40", draft.text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+        assertEquals("5.02", draft.text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE))
+        assertEquals("148", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("156", draft.text(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("520", draft.text(CardioLogField.CALORIES))
+        assertEquals("120", draft.text(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN))
+        assertEquals("178", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals(7, draft.effort)
+        assertEquals("hilly", draft.note)
+        assertEquals("", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("14:29", draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRequestCarriesTypedFieldsOnly() {
+        val draft =
+            blank(activity = CardioActivity.ROWER)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "29:54")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "7120")
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, "28")
+                .copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly")
+        val request = draft.toRequest()
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(7120.0, DistanceUnit.M), request.distance)
+        assertEquals(1794, request.durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(28, request.avgCadence)
+        assertEquals(6, request.effort)
+        assertNull(request.avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun logFieldOrderMatchesTheSheet() {
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields - CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+            CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE.logFields,
+        )
+        assertTrue(CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL.logFields.none { it == CardioLogField.AVG_PACE })
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun effortAndNoteTravelThroughCopy() {
+        val draft = blank().copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly")
+        assertEquals("6", draft.text(CardioLogField.EFFORT))
+        assertEquals("hilly", draft.text(CardioLogField.NOTE))
+        assertEquals("7", draft.with(CardioLogField.EFFORT, "7").text(CardioLogField.EFFORT))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun submitWaitsForADurationWithoutErrors() {
+        assertFalse(blank().canSubmit)
+        assertFalse(blank().with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "abc").canSubmit)
+        assertFalse(
+            blank()
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, "300")
+                .canSubmit,
+        )
+        assertTrue(blank().with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12").canSubmit)
+    }
+}

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlin.math.abs
+import kotlin.math.floor
+import kotlin.math.roundToLong
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+private val CLOCK_TEXT = Regex("^(\\d+):([0-5]\\d)(?::([0-5]\\d))?$")
+private const val MAX_DURATION_SECONDS = 86_400
+
+/**
+ * The log sheet's immutable draft: typed [texts] per field, [effort] and [note] as properties, and
+ * the [left] fields whose errors may show. Pure functions only — the 400 ms debounce lives in the
+ * sheet, not here.
+ *
+ * The range checks below it are file-private functions (not members) to stay under detekt's
+ * `TooManyFunctions` ceiling.
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity,
+    val distanceUnit: DistanceUnit,
+    val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val left: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+    val effort: Int? = null,
+    val note: String = "",
+) {
+    /** What the field holds: "" when nothing is typed. */
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> effort?.toString() ?: ""
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> note
+            else -> texts[field] ?: ""
+        }
+
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> copy(effort = value.trim().toIntOrNull())
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> copy(note = value)
+            else -> copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+        }
+
+    /** Marks the field as left, so its error may show. */
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(left = left + field)
+
+    /** [errors]' entry once the field was left, else null. */
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (field in left) errors()[field] else null
+
+    /** Every typed field held to the backend's ranges; an empty field is never in error. */
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> =
+        buildMap {
+            durationError()?.let { put(CardioLogField.DURATION, it) }
+            distanceError()?.let { put(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, it) }
+            paceError()?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, it) }
+            speedError()?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED, it) }
+            elevationError()?.let { put(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN, it) }
+            heartRateError(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE)?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, it) }
+            heartRateError(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE)?.let { put(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, it) }
+            maxBelowAvgError()?.let { put(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, it) }
+            cadenceError()?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, it) }
+            powerError()?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER, it) }
+            caloriesError()?.let { put(CardioLogField.CALORIES, it) }
+            if (effort != null && effort !in 1..10) put(CardioLogField.EFFORT, "between 1 and 10")
+        }
+
+    /** Duration parses and nothing is in error. */
+    val canSubmit: Boolean
+        get() = parseClock(raw(CardioLogField.DURATION)) != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    /**
+     * A blank pace/speed derived from duration and distance ("6:37", "31.9"); null when typed and
+     * always null for every other field.
+     */
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        val typed = raw(field)
+        return when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> {
+                if (typed.isNotEmpty()) return null
+                val seconds = CardioFormat.derivedPace(parseDuration(), parseDistance(), null, activity, distanceUnit)
+                seconds?.let { formatClock(it.toLong()) }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> {
+                if (typed.isNotEmpty()) return null
+                val speed = CardioFormat.derivedSpeed(parseDuration(), parseDistance(), null, distanceUnit)
+                speed?.let {
+                    val rounded = (it * 10).roundToLong()
+                    "${rounded / 10}.${abs(rounded % 10)}"
+                }
+            }
+
+            else -> {
+                null
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** Typed fields and effort; never the note, never a derived value. */
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest =
+        CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = parseDuration() ?: 0,
+            distance = parseDistance(),
+            avgPaceSeconds = parseClock(raw(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE)),
+            avgSpeed = parseDouble(raw(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED)),
+            avgHeartRate = parseInt(raw(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE)),
+            maxHeartRate = parseInt(raw(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE)),
+            calories = parseInt(raw(CardioLogField.CALORIES)),
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = parseInt(raw(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN)),
+            avgCadence = parseInt(raw(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE)),
+            avgPower = parseInt(raw(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER)),
+        )
+
+    companion object {
+        /**
+         * A blank draft; duration prefills from the clock — whole seconds of `now − startedAt` —
+         * while the workout is in progress.
+         */
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val started = workout.startedAt
+            val duration =
+                if (workout.status == WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS && started != null) {
+                    formatClock((now - started).inWholeSeconds.coerceAtLeast(0))
+                } else {
+                    ""
+                }
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                distanceUnit = workout.distanceUnit,
+                texts = if (duration.isEmpty()) emptyMap() else mapOf(CardioLogField.DURATION to duration),
+                note = workout.notes ?: "",
+            )
+        }
+
+        /** The saved log back as text with effort from it and the note from the workout's notes. */
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val log = workout.log
+            val texts =
+                buildMap {
+                    if (log == null) return@buildMap
+                    put(CardioLogField.DURATION, formatClock(log.durationSeconds.toLong()))
+                    log.distance?.let { put(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, formatDistance(it, workout.activity)) }
+                    log.avgPaceSeconds?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, formatClock(it.toLong())) }
+                    log.avgSpeed?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED, it.toString()) }
+                    log.elevationGain?.let { put(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN, it.toString()) }
+                    log.avgHeartRate?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, it.toString()) }
+                    log.maxHeartRate?.let { put(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, it.toString()) }
+                    log.avgCadence?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, it.toString()) }
+                    log.avgPower?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER, it.toString()) }
+                    log.calories?.let { put(CardioLogField.CALORIES, it.toString()) }
+                }
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                distanceUnit = workout.distanceUnit,
+                texts = texts,
+                effort = log?.effort,
+                note = workout.notes ?: "",
+            )
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.raw(field: CardioLogField): String = (texts[field] ?: "").trim()
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.parseDuration(): Int? = parseClock(raw(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.parseDistance(): CardioDistance? {
+    val value = parseDouble(raw(CardioLogField.DISTANCE))?.takeIf { it > 0 } ?: return null
+    return CardioDistance(value, if (isRower()) DistanceUnit.M else distanceUnit)
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.isRower(): Boolean = activity == CardioActivity.ROWER
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.speedUnit(): String = if (distanceUnit == DistanceUnit.MI) "mph" else "km/h"
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.elevationUnit(): String = if (distanceUnit == DistanceUnit.MI) "ft" else "m"
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.cadenceUnit(): String = if (activity.isRide) "rpm" else "spm"
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.paceUnit(): String = if (isRower()) "/500 m" else "/${distanceUnit.name.lowercase()}"
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.durationError(): String? {
+    val text = raw(CardioLogField.DURATION)
+    if (text.isEmpty()) return null
+    val seconds = parseClock(text) ?: return "use h:mm:ss"
+    if (seconds !in 1..MAX_DURATION_SECONDS) return "between 1 and $MAX_DURATION_SECONDS s"
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.distanceError(): String? {
+    val text = raw(CardioLogField.DISTANCE)
+    if (text.isEmpty()) return null
+    val value = parseDouble(text)
+    val unit = if (isRower()) DistanceUnit.M else distanceUnit
+    if (value == null || value <= 0 || value * unit.metres >= 1_000_000.0) {
+        return "between 0 and ${shortNumber(1_000_000.0 / unit.metres)} ${unit.name.lowercase()}"
+    }
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.paceError(): String? {
+    val text = raw(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE)
+    if (text.isEmpty()) return null
+    val seconds = parseClock(text) ?: return "use m:ss"
+    val max = if (isRower()) 600 else 3600
+    if (seconds !in 60..max) return "between ${formatClock(60)} and ${formatClock(max.toLong())} ${paceUnit()}"
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.speedError(): String? {
+    val text = raw(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED)
+    if (text.isEmpty()) return null
+    val value = parseDouble(text)
+    if (value == null || value !in 0.1..120.0) {
+        return "between ${shortNumber(0.1)} and ${shortNumber(120.0)} ${speedUnit()}"
+    }
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.elevationError(): String? {
+    val text = raw(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN)
+    if (text.isEmpty()) return null
+    val value = parseInt(text)
+    if (value == null || value !in 0..20_000) return "between 0 and 20000 ${elevationUnit()}"
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.heartRateError(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+    val text = raw(field)
+    if (text.isEmpty()) return null
+    val value = parseInt(text)
+    if (value == null || value !in 30..250) return "between 30 and 250 bpm"
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.maxBelowAvgError(): String? {
+    val avg = parseInt(raw(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE)) ?: return null
+    val maxText = raw(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE)
+    if (maxText.isEmpty()) return null
+    val max = parseInt(maxText) ?: return null
+    if (max < avg) return "max heart rate is below average"
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.cadenceError(): String? {
+    val text = raw(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE)
+    if (text.isEmpty()) return null
+    val value = parseInt(text)
+    val min = if (isRower()) 10 else 1
+    val max = if (isRower()) 60 else 250
+    if (value == null || value !in min..max) return "between $min and $max ${cadenceUnit()}"
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.powerError(): String? {
+    val text = raw(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER)
+    if (text.isEmpty()) return null
+    val value = parseInt(text)
+    if (value == null || value !in 1..2500) return "between 1 and 2500 W"
+    return null
+}
+
+private fun CardioLogDraft.caloriesError(): String? {
+    val text = raw(CardioLogField.CALORIES)
+    if (text.isEmpty()) return null
+    val value = parseInt(text)
+    if (value == null || value !in 0..20_000) return "between 0 and 20000 kcal"
+    return null
+}
+
+/** `m:ss` or `h:mm:ss` (minutes and seconds 00–59 after a colon) as whole seconds, else null. */
+private fun parseClock(text: String): Int? {
+    val match = CLOCK_TEXT.matchEntire(text.trim()) ?: return null
+    val (first, second, third) = match.destructured
+    val total =
+        if (third.isEmpty()) {
+            first.toLong() * 60 + second.toLong()
+        } else {
+            first.toLong() * 3600 + second.toLong() * 60 + third.toLong()
+        }
+    return total.coerceAtMost(Int.MAX_VALUE.toLong()).toInt()
+}
+
+private fun parseDouble(text: String): Double? = text.trim().toDoubleOrNull()?.takeIf { it.isFinite() }
+
+private fun parseInt(text: String): Int? = text.trim().toIntOrNull()
+
+/** `m:ss` under an hour, `h:mm:ss` from one up. */
+private fun formatClock(totalSeconds: Long): String {
+    val rest = totalSeconds % 3600
+    val short = "${rest / 60}:${(rest % 60).toString().padStart(2, '0')}"
+    return if (totalSeconds >= 3600) "${totalSeconds / 3600}:" + short.padStart(5, '0') else short
+}
+
+private fun formatDistance(
+    distance: CardioDistance,
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+): String {
+    if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) return (distance.value * distance.unit.metres).roundToLong().toString()
+    return shortNumber(distance.value)
+}
+
+/** A whole number without decimals, else rounded to two places. */
+private fun shortNumber(value: Double): String {
+    val rounded = (value * 100).roundToLong() / 100.0
+    return if (rounded == floor(rounded)) rounded.toLong().toString() else rounded.toString()
+}
